<script type="text/javascript">

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
  var el = document.querySelector("div.hide-seek-header-section");
  if (!el) { return; }
  var sync = function () {
    var y = window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
    if (y > 200) {
      el.classList.add("hide-seek-styles");
    } else {
      el.classList.remove("hide-seek-styles");
    }
  };
  window.addEventListener("scroll", sync, { passive: true });
  sync();
});</script>

														<h2 class="entry-title">
													<a href="https://pallacanestrocantu.com/cantu-lotta-ma-napoli-vince-80-99/">CANTÙ LOTTA, MA NAPOLI VINCE 80-99</a>
											</h2>
				
					<p class="post-meta"><span class="published">11 Ott 2026</span></p><div class="post-content"><div class="post-content-inner"><p>L’Acqua S.Bernardo prova fino all’ultimo a contendere i due punti a Napoli, ma nel quarto decisivo i partenopei riescono a trovare il break che vale la vittoria. LA CRONACA Coach Vitucci conferma il quintetto visto con Udine. Dowtin e Flagg segnano...</p>
</div></div>

<script type="text/javascript">

(function () {
  var SEL = '.shm-fullmenu .menu-item-has-children > a';
  var ARROW_ZONE = 56;   // px dalla destra della <a> considerati "freccia"
  var lock = 0;

  function busy() {
    var n = Date.now();
    if (n - lock < 500) { return true; }   // touchend + click dello stesso tocco
    lock = n;
    return false;
  }

  function handler(e) {
    var a = (e.target && e.target.closest) ? e.target.closest(SEL) : null;
    if (!a) { return; }

    var li = a.parentElement;
    if (!li) { return; }

    var href = a.getAttribute('href');
    var rect = a.getBoundingClientRect();

    var x = null;
    if (e.changedTouches && e.changedTouches.length) {
      x = e.changedTouches[0].clientX;
    } else if (typeof e.clientX === 'number' && e.clientX > 0) {
      x = e.clientX;
    }

    var onArrow = (x !== null) ? (rect.right - x) <= ARROW_ZONE : false;
    var noLink  = (!href || href === '#' || href.charAt(href.length - 1) === '#');
    var isOpen  = li.classList.contains('is-open');

    // tocco sul testo di una voce gia' aperta e con link vero -> lascia navigare
    if (!(onArrow || noLink || !isOpen)) { return; }

    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();     // blocca i gestori jQuery del tema e dello snippet touch
    if (busy()) { return; }

    var sibs = li.parentElement ? li.parentElement.children : [];
    for (var i = 0; i < sibs.length; i++) {
      if (sibs[i] !== li) { sibs[i].classList.remove('is-open'); }
    }
    if (isOpen) { li.classList.remove('is-open'); }
    else { li.classList.add('is-open'); }
  }

  document.addEventListener('touchend', handler, { capture: true, passive: false });
  document.addEventListener('click', handler, true);

  function markCurrent() {
    var items = document.querySelectorAll('.shm-fullmenu li.menu-item-has-children.current-menu-ancestor');
    for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) { items[i].classList.add('is-open'); }
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', markCurrent);
  } else {
    markCurrent();
  }
})();</script>
<!-- end Simple Custom CSS and JS -->
<!-- start Simple Custom CSS and JS -->
<script type="text/javascript">

jQuery(function ($) {
    if (!('ontouchstart' in window) && !(navigator.maxTouchPoints > 0)) return;
    var lock = 0;
    function busy() { var n = Date.now(); if (n - lock < 450) return true; lock = n; return false; }
    function openMenu() { $('body').addClass('open-menu'); $('.shm-burger').attr('aria-expanded', 'true'); $('#mobile-menu').attr('aria-hidden', 'false'); }
    function closeMenu() { $('body').removeClass('open-menu'); $('.shm-burger').attr('aria-expanded', 'false'); $('#mobile-menu').attr('aria-hidden', 'true'); }
    $(document).on('touchend', '.shm-header .shm-burger', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (busy()) return; $('body').hasClass('open-menu') ? closeMenu() : openMenu(); });
    $(document).on('touchend', '.shm-fullmenu .shm-close', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); if (busy()) return; closeMenu(); });
    $(document).on('touchend', '.shm-fullmenu .menu-item-has-children > a', function (e) { var href = $(this).attr('href'); if (!href || href === '#' || href.slice(-1) === '#') { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); var $li = $(this).parent(); $li.toggleClass('is-open').siblings('.is-open').removeClass('is-open'); } });
    $(document).on('touchend', '.shm-fullmenu a', function (e) { var $li = $(this).parent(); if ($li.hasClass('menu-item-has-children')) return; var href = $(this).attr('href'); if (!href || href.charAt(0) === '#') return; e.preventDefault(); closeMenu(); if ($(this).attr('target') === '_blank') { window.open(href, '_blank'); } else { window.location.href = href; } });
    $(document).on('touchend', '.shm-header .shm-search-form button', function (e) { var $f = $(this).closest('form'), $i = $f.find('input[type=search]'); if (!$f.hasClass('is-open') || !$.trim($i.val())) { e.preventDefault(); $f.addClass('is-open'); $i.focus(); } });
});
</script>
<!-- end Simple Custom CSS and JS -->
<!-- start Simple Custom CSS and JS -->
<script type="text/javascript">

jQuery(function ($) {
    if (!$('body').hasClass('search')) return;
    var $area = $('#left-area');
    if (!$area.length || $area.find('.shm-search-head').length) return;
    var q = $.trim(new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('s') || '');
    var esc = function (s) { return $('<div>').text(s).html(); };
    var $posts = $area.children('article');
    var n = $posts.length;
    var hasMore = $area.children('.pagination').find('a').length > 0;
    var sub = n ? (n + (n === 1 ? ' risultato trovato' : ' risultati trovati') + (hasMore ? ' in questa pagina' : '')) : 'Nessun risultato: prova con un altro termine';
    $area.prepend(
        '<div class="shm-search-head">' +
        '<h1>Risultati per <span>&ldquo;' + esc(q) + '&rdquo;</span></h1>' +
        '<p>' + sub + '</p>' +
        '<form role="search" method="get" action="' + window.location.origin + '/">' +
        '<input type="search" name="s" value="' + esc(q) + '" placeholder="Cerca nel sito..." required>' +
        '<button type="submit">Cerca</button></form></div>'
    );
    $posts.each(function () {
        var $a = $(this);
        var href = $a.find('.entry-title a').attr('href');
        var $meta = $a.children('.post-meta');
        var date = $.trim($meta.find('.published').text());
        if (date) { $meta.text(date); } else { $meta.remove(); }
        var txt = '';
        $a.contents().filter(function () { return this.nodeType === 3; }).each(function () { txt += this.nodeValue; $(this).remove(); });
        var $body = $('<div class="shm-card-body"></div>');
        $body.append($a.children('.post-meta'), $a.children('.entry-title'));
        $a.children('.post-content, .excerpt').each(function () { txt += ' ' + $(this).text(); $(this).remove(); });
        txt = $.trim(txt).replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
        if (txt) $body.append($('<p class="shm-excerpt"></p>').text(txt));
        if (href) $body.append('<a class="shm-more" href="' + href + '">Leggi tutto</a>');
        $a.append($body);
        if (!$a.find('.entry-featured-image-url').length) $a.addClass('shm-no-thumb');
    });
    // caso "nessun risultato": nascondo il blocco standard di Divi (titolo + form), c'è già il nostro
    $area.children('.entry, .no-results').each(function () { $(this).find('form, h1, h2').hide(); });
});
</script>
<!-- end Simple Custom CSS and JS -->
<!-- start Simple Custom CSS and JS -->
<script type="text/javascript">

jQuery(function($){
  $(document).on('click', '.shm-header .shm-search-form:not(.shm-search-fullmenu) button', function(e){
    var $f = $(this).closest('form'), $i = $f.find('input[type=search]');
    if (!$f.hasClass('is-open') || !$.trim($i.val())) { e.preventDefault(); $f.addClass('is-open'); $i.focus(); }
  });
  $(document).on('click', function(e){
    if (!$(e.target).closest('.shm-search-form').length) { $('.shm-search-form.is-open').each(function(){ if(!$.trim($(this).find('input').val())) $(this).removeClass('is-open'); }); }
  });
});
</script>
<!-- end Simple Custom CSS and JS -->
